# Outbe Protocol Parameters

Every protocol, economic and network parameter defined in the outbe-chain code: its value, what it controls, how it can change, and the line that defines it. Read from commit [`4c2a09f`](https://github.com/outbe/outbe-chain/tree/4c2a09f67c50a9b36990182a993c55e5b6c339aa); line links are relative to the repository root and may drift as the code changes.

**1,218 parameters** across 10 domains. Rows marked **★** are headline parameters; **⚠** marks a note that flags a mismatch or stale value.

**How a value can change** — **Constant**: compiled in, needs a release. **Genesis**: set in the genesis file, or a compiled default only test builds may override from `config.outbeProtocol`. **On-chain**: held in chain state, changed by governance or an admin role. **Config**: default of an off-chain binary. **Solidity**: constant or immutable in a contract. **Derived**: computed from other parameters.

## Headline parameters

### Floor, call and settlement · mainnet profile

| Parameter | Value | Detail |
|---|---|---|
| Floor price | **1.08 × entry** | +8% over the reference-currency entry price. Same for Nod, Intex and Gem. |
| Qualification | **1 day above floor** | One finalized UTC-day COEN price above the floor opens settlement. No holding period in code. |
| Call price · Intex, Gem | **2.28 × entry** | +128%. The canon states 1.64×. |
| Call price · Nod | **3.56 × entry** | +256%. Nod buckets are called and forfeited like Intex and Gem. |
| Call price · Credis | **1.64 × anchor** | +64% over the previous day's COEN price when the position opens. |
| Call trigger | **21 of 28 days** | UTC days with the daily COEN price strictly above the call price. The canon states 21 of 30. |
| Call notice | **7 days** | Time to settle after a call. Unsettled rights are forfeited and their load returns to the Promis pool. |
| SRA Gem cost | **64%** | SRA-class Gems settle at 64% of the full Cost Amount; other Gem types pay 100%. |

### Daily emission and reward pools

| Parameter | Value | Detail |
|---|---|---|
| Emission limit, day 0 | **268,435,456 COEN** | 2²⁸ COEN per UTC day at genesis. |
| Peak emission | **581.6M COEN** | Reached on day 1,024 (26 · 2²⁶ / 3), then the limit falls. |
| Floor emission | **67,108,864 COEN** | 2²⁶ COEN per day from day 3,072, permanently. |
| Validator, WAA, SRA, CCA | **4% each** | Fixed shares of each day's limit, rounded down. |
| Metadosis share | **84%** | The rest of the day's limit, plus rounding dust and unspent pool amounts. |
| Per-address cap · WAA, SRA | **32% of a pool** | No single address takes more than 32% of a day's pool; the excess is redistributed in up to 10 passes. |
| CCA reward weight | **Pro rata, no cap** | Net Gratis originated per UTC day. |
| CCA bond | **1,000,000,000 COEN** | Required to be an active CCA. 128-day unbond cooldown; at most 128 active CCAs. |

### Allocation · Metadosis, Lysis, Desis, Fidelity

| Parameter | Value | Detail |
|---|---|---|
| Symbolic rate | **32% of nominal** | Share of Tribute nominal that becomes Nod-side Gratis demand. |
| Intex-side ceiling | **68% of nominal** | Derived: nominal minus the Lysis Limit, on GREEN days. |
| League ceiling fmax | **64%** | 2 × the day's average Gratis fraction; it scales the league curve and is not a hard clamp. |
| RED day | **÷ 8** | Nod-side demand and day limit divided by 8 (32% → 4% of nominal); the Intex side gets 0. |
| Day colour | **GREEN if price rose** | GREEN when the day's COEN/USD VWAP is above the previous day's; otherwise RED. |
| WorldwideDay pipeline | **614 h** | 50 h forming + 502 h lookback + 50 h offering + 12 h waiting. |
| Fidelity leagues | **4,096** | League = 1 + ⌊RCFI × 4096 / RCFI max⌋, capped at 4,096. |
| Fidelity half-life | **365 days** | Decayed time saturates at H / ln 2 ≈ 526.6 days. |

### Chain, validators and governance

| Parameter | Value | Detail |
|---|---|---|
| Chain IDs | **676 · 54,322,345** | Mainnet 676, testnet 54,322,345, devnet 424,242. Nodes accept no others. |
| Block time | **2 s minimum** | Leader timeout 4 s, certification 8 s. Gas limit 30,000,000 per block; minimum base fee 7 wei. |
| Finality quorum | **⌊2n/3⌋ + 1** | Signers needed to notarize and finalize. Finality is deterministic, with no confirmation depth. |
| Validator stake | **100,000 COEN min** | At most 33% of total stake per validator; 21-day unbonding. Up to 128 validators. |
| Slashing | **5% felony** | 10% of it goes to the evidence submitter. A missed OCOMP vote or enclave upgrade costs 10%. |
| Governance | **⅔ of active validators** | Voting window up to 86,400 blocks. Upgrades activate 100 blocks after approval. |
| Oracle | **⌈2N/3⌉ quorum** | Votes every 2 blocks, median price, 8 h VWAP for settlement reads. |
| Zero-fee transactions | **8 per day** | Sponsored transactions per signer per UTC day, up to 500,000 gas each. |

## Emission, rewards & validators

The daily emission limit and its decay, the fixed reward pools taken off the top, agent-reward distribution, staking, slashing, the validator set, zero-fee policy and on-chain votes.

Scope: `crates/system/{emissionlimit, cycle, accounting, rewards, staking, validatorset, slashindicator, zerofee, vote, update} · crates/core/{agentreward, ccaregistry}`

### Emission curve

| Parameter | Value | How set | Code | Source | What it controls |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

Protocol-to-native conversion multiplies by 10^12 and native-to-protocol floor-divides by 10^12.<br>_Detail:_ Shared unit constants: crates/blockchain/primitives/src/units.rs:30 (PROTOCOL_AMOUNT_DECIMALS), :33 (NATIVE_UNITS_PER_PROTOCOL_UNIT), :42 (NATIVE_TOKEN_DECIMALS). The independent vector file covers every day 0..=3072. |
| ★ Daily emission curve (two-phase logistic) | Rises 2^28 -> P over days 0-1024, falls P -> 2^26 over days 1024-3072, then flat at 2^26 (not a halving schedule) <br>_COEN per UTC day_ | Derived | `d<=1024: E = O1 + A/(1+e^((512-d)/128)); 1024<d<3072: E = O2 - D/(1+e^((2048-d)/384)); A=(P-2^28)/tanh(2), O1=(2^28+P)/2-A/2, D=(P-2^26)/tanh(4/3), O2=(P+2^26)/2+D/2; result floored to 1e-6 COEN, clamped >= floor` | [`crates/system/emissionlimit/src/day_emission.rs:102`](../crates/system/emissionlimit/src/day_emission.rs#L102) | day_emission_limit(day_number) is a closed-form, storage-free function of whole UTC days since genesis: a logistic rise centred on day 512 (scale K1 = 128 days) followed by an inverted logistic fall centred on day 2048 (scale K2 = 384 days). Both phases meet exactly at P on day 1024 and the fall reaches 2^26 on day 3072.<br>_Detail:_ Constants evaluate to A ~ 324,860,725.09, O1 ~ 262,592,442.79, D ~ 591,338,882.39, O2 ~ 620,028,950.53 COEN. Midpoints: day 512 = (2^28+P)/2 = 425,022,805.333333; day 2048 = (P+2^26)/2 = 324,359,509.333333. | Phase-2 midpoint day | Day 2,048 <br>_days since genesis UTC day_ | Constant | `PHASE_TWO_MIDPOINT_DAY: u32 = 2_048` | [`crates/system/emissionlimit/src/day_emission.rs:37`](../crates/system/emissionlimit/src/day_emission.rs#L37) | Inflection of the fall; the cap on day 2048 is the average of P and 2^26 (324,359,509.333333 COEN). |

PIN_DAY_3072 = 67_108_864_000_000 (six-decimal units)` | [`crates/system/emissionlimit/src/day_emission.rs:144`](../crates/system/emissionlimit/src/day_emission.rs#L144) | Production output is pinned at 14 checkpoint days and compared, for every day 0-3072, with an independently generated Decimal reference vector.<br>_Detail:_ Other pins: d1 268,480,452.713195; d512 425,022,805.333333; d1024 581,610,154.666666; d1025 581,516,499.768072; d2048 324,359,509.333333; d2919 84,150,920.679186; d3071 67,202,518.898594; d3072 67,108,864. ## Metadosis & genesis protocol constants

The top-tier allocator that splits the day's emission between the Nod path and the Intex path, its WorldwideDay pipeline timing, and the protocol constants a genesis file can address.

Scope: `crates/core/metadosis · crates/blockchain/constants`

### Genesis-overridable

| Parameter | Value | How set | Code | Source | What it controls |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| ★ Metadosis forming period | 50 h (180,000 s) <br>_seconds_ | Genesis | `DEFAULT_METADOSIS_FORMING_PERIOD_SECONDS: u64 = 50 * 60 * 60` | [`crates/blockchain/constants/src/lib.rs:12`](../crates/blockchain/constants/src/lib.rs#L12) | Length of a WorldwideDay's FORMING window: forming_end = forming_start + this value, where forming_start is 00:00 of the WWD date in UTC+14. The Oracle requires the WWD VWAP snapshot used for GREEN/RED classification to span exactly this window.<br>_Detail:_ Override key metadosis.formingPeriodSeconds; validation: non-zero and <= production default (180,000) (crates/blockchain/constants/src/lib.rs:272). Overrides are read from genesis config.outbeProtocol only by builds with the `test-protocol-overrides` feature; production builds fail startup if config.outbeProtocol is present at all (crates/blockchain/constants/src/lib.rs:380, called from bin/outbe-chain/src/cli/chain_spec.rs:65). Runtime read: crates/core/metadosis/src/lifecycle.rs:101; Oracle window check: crates/system/oracle/src/runtime.rs:672. 50 h is the span during which a calendar date exists somewhere on Earth (UTC+14 midnight to UTC-12 midnight). |
| ★ Metadosis lookback delay | 502 h (1,807,200 s, ~20.9 days) <br>_seconds_ | Genesis | `DEFAULT_METADOSIS_LOOKBACK_DELAY_SECONDS: u64 = 502 * 60 * 60` | [`crates/blockchain/constants/src/lib.rs:13`](../crates/blockchain/constants/src/lib.rs#L13) | Gap between the end of FORMING and the opening of OFFERING (status LOOKBACK_DELAY): lookback_end = forming_end + this value. Tributes for the WWD cannot be offered until lookback_end.<br>_Detail:_ Override key metadosis.lookbackDelaySeconds; validation: <= production default only, so 0 is allowed (the only Metadosis window that may be zero) (crates/blockchain/constants/src/lib.rs:283). Overrides are read from genesis config.outbeProtocol only by builds with the `test-protocol-overrides` feature; production builds fail startup if config.outbeProtocol is present at all (crates/blockchain/constants/src/lib.rs:380, called from bin/outbe-chain/src/cli/chain_spec.rs:65). Code comment calls it '~21 days' (crates/core/metadosis/src/constants.rs:9). network.example.yaml states the live testnet overrides it to 0 (scripts/network.example.yaml:111), which requires a test-protocol-overrides binary; the localnet E2E harness also uses 0 (testing/e2e-harness/src/world/localnet/bootstrap.rs:42). |
| ★ Metadosis offering period | 50 h (180,000 s) <br>_seconds_ | Genesis | `DEFAULT_METADOSIS_OFFERING_PERIOD_SECONDS: u64 = 50 * 60 * 60` | [`crates/blockchain/constants/src/lib.rs:14`](../crates/blockchain/constants/src/lib.rs#L14) | Length of the OFFERING window during which Tributes may be offered for the WWD: offering_end = lookback_end + this value. The WWD's Tribute partition is unsealed when OFFERING opens and sealed when it closes.<br>_Detail:_ Override key metadosis.offeringPeriodSeconds; validation: non-zero and <= production default (crates/blockchain/constants/src/lib.rs:288). Overrides are read from genesis config.outbeProtocol only by builds with the `test-protocol-overrides` feature; production builds fail startup if config.outbeProtocol is present at all (crates/blockchain/constants/src/lib.rs:380, called from bin/outbe-chain/src/cli/chain_spec.rs:65). Unseal/seal effects: crates/core/metadosis/src/lifecycle/effects.rs:59 / crates/core/metadosis/src/lifecycle/effects.rs:63. Localnet E2E default 120 s (testing/e2e-harness/src/world/localnet/bootstrap.rs:43). |
| Metadosis waiting period | 12 h (43,200 s) <br>_seconds_ | Genesis | `DEFAULT_METADOSIS_WAITING_PERIOD_SECONDS: u64 = 12 * 60 * 60` | [`crates/blockchain/constants/src/lib.rs:15`](../crates/blockchain/constants/src/lib.rs#L15) | Delay between the close of OFFERING and READY processing: scheduled_process_time = offering_end + this value; a WAITING WWD becomes READY once block time >= scheduled_process_time.<br>_Detail:_ Override key metadosis.waitingPeriodSeconds; validation: non-zero and <= production default (crates/blockchain/constants/src/lib.rs:293). Overrides are read from genesis config.outbeProtocol only by builds with the `test-protocol-overrides` feature; production builds fail startup if config.outbeProtocol is present at all (crates/blockchain/constants/src/lib.rs:380, called from bin/outbe-chain/src/cli/chain_spec.rs:65). READY rule: crates/core/metadosis/src/reducer.rs:358. Localnet E2E uses 30 s (testing/e2e-harness/src/world/localnet/bootstrap.rs:44). |
| Metadosis advance interval (ProtocolCycle period) | 1 h (3,600 s) <br>_seconds_ | Genesis | `DEFAULT_METADOSIS_ADVANCE_INTERVAL_SECONDS: u64 = 60 * 60` | [`crates/blockchain/constants/src/lib.rs:16`](../crates/blockchain/constants/src/lib.rs#L16) | Period of the Cycle ProtocolCycle trigger (aligned to the unix epoch) that runs start_metadosis: WWD creation, reducer advancement of all active WWDs, and processing of at most one READY WWD per tick.<br>_Detail:_ Override key metadosis.advanceIntervalSeconds; validation: non-zero and <= production default (crates/blockchain/constants/src/lib.rs:298); no 'divides a day' rule. Overrides are read from genesis config.outbeProtocol only by builds with the `test-protocol-overrides` feature; production builds fail startup if config.outbeProtocol is present at all (crates/blockchain/constants/src/lib.rs:380, called from bin/outbe-chain/src/cli/chain_spec.rs:65). Consumed at crates/system/cycle/src/triggers.rs:171. Duplicated by the unlinked compile-time constant WWD_ADVANCE_TICK_CADENCE_HOURS = 1 (crates/core/metadosis/src/constants.rs:23). ## Lysis, Desis, Fidelity, Gratis & Promis

How the Nod-side share is spread across Tributes (Lysis, Fidelity and League weighted), how the Intex-side share is spread (Desis), and the Gratis and Promis mining-right rules.

Scope: `crates/core/{lysis, desis, fidelity, fidelity-math, promislimit, gratis, gratisfactory, promis, promisfactory}`

### Lysis distribution

| Parameter | Value | How set | Code | Source | What it controls |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Lysis fixed-point scale | 10^6 <br>_fixed-point denominator (1.0 = 1,000,000)_ | Constant | `pub(crate) const SCALE: U256 = SCALE_1E6_U256; // SCALE_U128 = SCALE_1E6_U128` | [`crates/core/lysis/src/algorithm.rs:7`](../crates/core/lysis/src/algorithm.rs#L7) | All Lysis league shares, tau masses and Gratis fractions are integers on a 10^6 scale; a Tribute's Gratis load is nominal x fraction / 10^6.<br>_Detail:_ Independent reference uses the same scale: SCALE_DECIMAL = "1000000" (testing/lysis-v1-reference/src/lib.rs:25). |
| ★ Day-average Gratis fraction f | Lysis Limit / total Tribute nominal (<= 32% GREEN, <= 4% RED) <br>_fraction of Tribute nominal (x10^6)_ | Derived | `let f = scaled_floor(lysis_limit_minor, SCALE, total_interest)?;` | [`crates/core/lysis/src/program_v1/execute.rs:372`](../crates/core/lysis/src/program_v1/execute.rs#L372) | Target nominal-weighted average share of Tribute nominal loaded as Gratis into Nods, derived each day as floor(Lysis Limit x 10^6 / sum of Tribute nominal). It is the spending ceiling the league fractions are normalised against.<br>_Detail:_ Not a constant in Lysis: the Lysis Limit is frozen by Metadosis (out of scope) as min(floor(nominal x SYMBOLIC_RATE 32 / 100), day Gratis limit), with both divided by RED_DAY_REDUCTION_COEF = 8 on RED days (crates/core/metadosis/src/settlement.rs, constants.rs:26,29) - hence f <= 0.32 GREEN, <= 0.04 RED. The OCOMP planner refuses a zero Lysis Limit (crates/core/lysis/src/program_v1/planner.rs:267). Reference: testing/lysis-v1-reference/src/lib.rs:403. |
| ★ Per-league maximum fraction fmax | 2 x f (64% when f = 32%) <br>_fraction of Tribute nominal (x10^6)_ | Derived | `let fmax = f.checked_mul(U256::from(2_u8))` | [`crates/core/lysis/src/program_v1/execute.rs:373`](../crates/core/lysis/src/program_v1/execute.rs#L373) | Scale of the league distribution: every league fraction is computed as fmax times a tail sum of tau masses, and beta is solved against f/fmax = 0.5. fmax is always twice the day-average fraction f.<br>_Detail:_ Canon calls this the 'per-Tribute maximum 64%' (2 x 32%). Code has no explicit clamp of a league fraction at fmax; only the two aggregate normalisations bound spending, and unit tests assert only fraction <= 2 x fmax. With c = 0.2 fixing the end masses at 10%/40%, a float model of the same formula never exceeded ~0.917 x fmax (~58.7% at f = 32%) - analysis, not a code constant. Reference: testing/lysis-v1-reference/src/lib.rs:404 ("fmax-two-f" vector in crates/core/lysis/vectors/lysis-v1/manifest.json). |
| Symbolic share used by Lysis tests (f / fmax) | f = 32%, fmax = 64% <br>_fraction (x10^6)_ | Constant | `const F_FP_DEFAULT: U256 = u256_from_u128(SCALE_U128 * 32 / 100); const F_MAX_FP: U256 = u256_from_u128(SCALE_U128 * 64 / 100);` | [`crates/core/lysis/src/tests.rs:7`](../crates/core/lysis/src/tests.rs#L7) | Illustrative day whose Gratis allocation equals the 32% symbolic share of nominal, with the per-league ceiling at twice that; used only to exercise the distribution in tests.<br>_Detail:_ Test-only (doc comment: 'Production derives both from the day ... these are test inputs, not policy'). Duplicated at crates/core/lysis/src/algorithm.rs:330-331 and testing/lysis-v1-reference/src/lib.rs:923-924. 32% mirrors Metadosis SYMBOLIC_RATE = 32 (crates/core/metadosis/src/constants.rs:26). tests.rs also mentions a 'historical 8%/16%' fraction range that the current logic no longer applies (deprecated). |
| Tau rank exponent a | 0.2 (x^(1/5)) <br>_exponent_ | Constant | `const POLICY_A_NUM: u32 = 1; const POLICY_A_DEN: u32 = 5;` | [`crates/core/lysis/src/algorithm.rs:19`](../crates/core/lysis/src/algorithm.rs#L19) | Numerator of each middle tau weight is (i - 0.5)^(1/5), where i is the rank boundary between adjacent present leagues ordered highest league first.<br>⚠ fp_root ignores the numerator (_p); only p = 1 is supported. Doc comment at algorithm.rs:16-17 ('fixed-point, denominator = 1000 ... a=0.2 -> 200/1000') is stale: the code uses integer q-th roots, not a /1000 representation. Reference: testing/lysis-v1-reference/src/lib.rs:236. |

The cumulative share's final value is also forced to 1.0.<br>_Detail:_ Cumulative forcing at crates/core/lysis/src/algorithm.rs:177-178. Reference: testing/lysis-v1-reference/src/lib.rs:398-402. |

A zero total nominal or an empty Tribute set is also a hard failure (execute.rs:52-54, 73-75). |

| Lysis entry price source | Previous UTC day VWAP, frozen once per WorldwideDay <br>_COEN price per reference currency (6 dec)_ | Constant | `let previous_day = previous_date_key(timestamp_to_date_key(process_time)); ... get_utc_day_vwap_for_iso(storage, previous_day, iso)` | [`crates/core/lysis/src/runtime.rs:55`](../crates/core/lysis/src/runtime.rs#L55) | For each reference currency, the finalized VWAP of the UTC day before the WorldwideDay's scheduled processing time is frozen once and reused; currencies without a VWAP are omitted, and a Tribute whose reference currency has no frozen price aborts the run (EntryPriceUnavailable).<br>_Detail:_ Snapshot stored via outbe_nod::api::store_entry_price_snapshot; later calls return the frozen map (runtime.rs:52-54). |
| ★ Intex-side contributor weight | Per-owner sum of eligible Tribute nominal (Fidelity-independent) <br>_nominal minor units_ | Derived | `if !tribute.exclude_from_intex_issuance { *contributor = contributor.checked_add(tribute.nominal_amount_minor) }` | [`crates/core/lysis/src/program_v1/execute.rs:263`](../crates/core/lysis/src/program_v1/execute.rs#L263) | Lysis records, per owner, the summed nominal of Tributes not flagged exclude_from_intex_issuance; this certified contributor root is the weight for the day's Intex-side proceeds and does not depend on league. Excluded Tributes still receive a Nod.<br>_Detail:_ Pro-rata payout happens in IntexFactory pay_contributor_batch (crates/core/intexfactory/src/runtime.rs:431+, out of scope). Canon describes Desis as distributing the Intex side 'proportional to Tribute value, Fidelity-independent'; in code that proportionality is this Lysis contributor map plus the IntexFactory payout, while Desis itself runs the Intex auction. |

| ★ Minimum Intex bid quantity floor | 4% (400 bps) of the prior clearing's issued count <br>_basis points_ | Constant | `pub const BID_QUANTITY_FLOOR_BPS: u32 = 400;  derived = prev_issued * prev_load * 400 / (10_000 * today_load), min(u16::MAX)` | [`crates/core/desis/src/constants.rs:4`](../crates/core/desis/src/constants.rs#L4) | Bids for fewer Intex than floor(prev issued x prev PROMIS load x 400 / (10,000 x today's load)) are skipped at clearing; the floor is restated at today's load so a ladder step does not push it above the day's supply. 0 when no day has cleared yet.<br>_Detail:_ Derivation at crates/core/desis/src/runtime.rs:246-268; capped at 65,535 (runtime.rs:266). |
| ★ Daily Intex supply | floor(Desis Limit / PROMIS load), <= 2^32 - 1 <br>_Intex units_ | Derived | `let supply_intex = (desis_limit_minor / config.promis_load_minor).min(u128::from(u32::MAX)) as u32;` | [`crates/core/desis/src/runtime.rs:579`](../crates/core/desis/src/runtime.rs#L579) | The number of Intex units the day can issue is the Desis Limit divided by the per-Intex PROMIS load, floored; the remainder is conversion dust returned to PromisLimit after clearing. |
| ★ Uniform-rate clearing rule | Clearing rate = last allocated bid's rate <br>_rule_ | Constant | `sort: b.intex_bid_rate.cmp(&a.intex_bid_rate).then_with(\|\| a.timestamp.cmp(&b.timestamp));  clearing_rate = bid.intex_bid_rate;` | [`crates/core/desis/src/runtime.rs:1183`](../crates/core/desis/src/runtime.rs#L1183) | Bids are ranked by descending rate then ascending timestamp (stable, ties keep chain order) and filled until supply runs out, with at most one partial fill at the margin; every winner pays the last allocated bid's rate and is refunded locked minus paid.<br>_Detail:_ Sort at runtime.rs:1119-1125; skip rules at runtime.rs:1163-1168. One Intex series is issued per distinct (issuance, reference) currency pair among winners (runtime.rs:1293-1341). |
| Minimum Intex bid rate | 0 (no floor) <br>_bid rate (x10^6)_ | Constant | `min_intex_bid_rate: 0,  // `min_intex_bid_rate = 0` means no bid floor` | [`crates/core/desis/src/schema.rs:88`](../crates/core/desis/src/schema.rs#L88) | Bids below this rate are skipped and it is the initial clearing rate; production always sets it to 0, so no rate floor applies.<br>_Detail:_ No other assignment exists in crates/; still relayed as minIntexBidRate in AUCTION_STAGE_START. |

Fallback at crates/core/desis/src/runtime.rs:162-170. |
| ★ Launch PROMIS load per Intex | 10^11 minor = 100,000 PROMIS <br>_exponent of PROMIS minor units_ | Constant | `pub const PROMIS_LOAD_LAUNCH_EXPONENT: u32 = 11;` | [`crates/core/desis/src/constants.rs:10`](../crates/core/desis/src/constants.rs#L10) | Rung carried by the first Intex: each Intex unit is loaded with 10^11 PROMIS minor units (100,000 PROMIS at 6 decimals); the launch anchor is 11 + the digit count of the first priced COEN/USD rate.<br>_Detail:_ Compile-time assertion that the launch rung equals 100,000 PROMIS: crates/core/desis/src/runtime.rs:93-97. |
| ★ PROMIS load ladder formula | load = 10^(anchor_digits - digits(COEN/USD rate)) <br>_PROMIS minor units per Intex_ | Derived | `launch_anchor_digits(rate) = PROMIS_LOAD_LAUNCH_EXPONENT + decimal_digits(rate); anchor_exponent = anchor_digits.saturating_sub(decimal_digits(rate)).min(21)` | [`crates/core/desis/src/runtime.rs:128`](../crates/core/desis/src/runtime.rs#L128) | The per-Intex PROMIS load steps by whole decades opposite to the COEN/USD rate, so load x rate stays near its launch value (e.g. launch at 0.001 USD: 10^12 at 0.0001, 10^11 at 0.001, 10^10 at 0.01). A step emits PromisLoadStepped and rescales the minimum-bid floor.<br>_Detail:_ Anchor digits captured once on the first USD-priced start (runtime.rs:171-178); stored exponent 0 means unset. Escrow basis per Intex equals this load (crates/core/desis/src/schema.rs:105-107). |
| PROMIS load ladder deadband | +/-2% (200 bps) at each decade boundary <br>_basis points_ | Constant | `pub const PROMIS_LOAD_DEADBAND_BPS: u32 = 200;  lo = 10^(d-1) * (10_000 - 200); hi = 10^d * (10_000 + 200); hold if lo <= rate*10_000 < hi` | [`crates/core/desis/src/constants.rs:13`](../crates/core/desis/src/constants.rs#L13) | The current rung is held while the rate stays inside its decade widened by 2% on each side, so a rate hovering at a boundary does not flip the load daily (e.g. steps down only at >= 1.02x the boundary, back up only below 0.98x).<br>_Detail:_ Applied at crates/core/desis/src/runtime.rs:137-140 (edges compared scaled up to survive integer division). |
| PROMIS load override | None (production); Some(1) under e2e-test <br>_PROMIS minor units per Intex_ | Config | `#[cfg(not(feature = "e2e-test"))] pub const PROMIS_LOAD_OVERRIDE: Option<u128> = None;  #[cfg(feature = "e2e-test")] ... = Some(1);` | [`crates/core/desis/src/constants.rs:18`](../crates/core/desis/src/constants.rs#L18) | Fixed load bypassing the ladder; only the e2e-test build sets it (1 minor unit) because the harness seeds a 500 PROMIS mint cap.<br>_Detail:_ e2e value at constants.rs:20; test a_production_build_has_no_load_override pins None. |

## Nod, Intex, Gem, Tribute & PayNote

Qualification (Floor Price) and Call thresholds, notice periods, auctions, Gem fragmentation, Tribute limits and the shielded PayNote pool.

Scope: `crates/core/{nod, nodfactory, intex, intexfactory, gem, gemfactory, tribute, tributefactory, paynote}`

### Qualification & floor

| Parameter | Value | How set | Code | Source | What it controls |

| ★ Intex floor price multiplier | 1.08× entry (+8%) <br>_percentage points over entry price (PRICE_RATE_DEN = 100)_ | Genesis | `FLOOR_RATE: u16 = 8; floor_price_minor = marked_up(entry_price_minor, cfg.floor_rate) = entry × (PRICE_RATE_DEN + 8) / PRICE_RATE_DEN` | [`crates/core/intexfactory/src/constants.rs:55`](../crates/core/intexfactory/src/constants.rs#L55) | Sets each Intex series' Floor Price (reference-currency minor units) at issuance; the series becomes settleable once a finalized COEN/<reference> daily VWAP closes above it.<br>_Detail:_ PROD-profile value. DEV profile uses floor_rate 5 (1.05×), crates/core/intexfactory/src/config.rs:56. Applied at crates/core/intexfactory/src/runtime.rs:53 and read by Desis for auction-stage prices. Matches canon (reference entry × 1.08). Integer division rounds the floor down. |

Computed in crates/core/gemfactory/src/runtime.rs:725-730. Matches canon 1.08×. |

Formula at crates/core/nod/src/schema.rs:394-398; certified materialization rejects a Nod whose floor differs from this derivation (crates/core/nodfactory/src/materialization.rs:183). Matches canon 1.08×. |
| ★ Qualification test (Floor) | Any one finalized UTC-day COEN/ref VWAP strictly above Floor Price, counted from the first full UTC day after issuance; no minimum elapsed time <br>_finalized UTC-day VWAP, six decimals_ | Derived | `outbe_oracle::api::closed_above_floor(storage, reference_currency, floor_price_minor, first_full_day(issued_at))` | [`crates/core/nod/src/api.rs:37`](../crates/core/nod/src/api.rs#L37) | Nod, Intex and Gem qualification is derived on read and never stored: a right is settleable once the highest finalized daily VWAP of COEN/<reference currency> since its first full UTC day exceeds its Floor Price. Because the test is a max-since check, qualification persists once reached.<br>⚠ Same rule for Intex (crates/core/intexfactory/src/runtime.rs:896) and Gem (crates/core/gem/src/api.rs:67). CANON MISMATCH: canon requires '21 days elapsed AND globally-formed COEN/ref > Floor Price'; code has no 21-day elapsed requirement — a single qualifying finalized UTC day suffices, so the earliest settlement is right after the first full UTC day following issuance is finalized above the floor. |
| Day-count start (first full UTC day) | Issuance UTC day if issued exactly at 00:00:00 UTC, otherwise the next UTC day <br>_UTC calendar day (yyyymmdd)_ | Constant | `first_full_day(issued_at)` | [`crates/core/nod/src/api.rs:45`](../crates/core/nod/src/api.rs#L45) | Prices from days before a right's first full UTC day are ignored by both the qualification test and the Call breach count, so a partial issuance day never counts.<br>_Detail:_ Helper defined in crates/blockchain/primitives/src/time.rs:91 (outside scope). Also used at crates/core/nod/src/called.rs:334, crates/core/intexfactory/src/called.rs:403, crates/core/intexfactory/src/runtime.rs:904, crates/core/gem/src/api.rs:72 and crates/core/gem/src/schema.rs:47. Nod buckets use the issued_at sealed from the bucket's first member. |
| Threshold price series | Finalized UTC-day VWAP of COEN/<reference ISO>; scans use the last fully closed UTC day <br>_six-decimal reference-currency minor units_ | Constant | `closed_day(): previous_date_key(timestamp_to_date_key(block.timestamp)); skipped while oracle.utc_day_vwap_last_finalized < that day` | [`crates/core/nod/src/called.rs:118`](../crates/core/nod/src/called.rs#L118) | Floor and Call checks compare against the Oracle's finalized per-UTC-day VWAP for the contract's reference currency; the daily scans act only on the most recent fully closed UTC day once the Oracle has finalized it.<br>_Detail:_ Same gate in crates/core/intexfactory/src/called.rs:71 and crates/core/gem/src/hooks.rs:37. A day with no published price neither qualifies nor counts as a breach. Prices are UTC-day based here, whereas Tribute pricing uses Worldwide-Day VWAPs. |

## Credis, Vault, stablecoins & governance

Credis routing and Gratis pledges, the VaultRouter, Rust-native stablecoin policy, the governance registry of canon and proposals, and shared encodings.

Scope: `crates/core/{credis, credisfactory, vaultrouter, stablecoin, stablecoinpolicy, stablecoinfactory, governance, common, compressed-entities}`

### Credis

| Parameter | Value | How set | Code | Source | What it controls |

| Burn authorization | burn: ISSUER burns own balance (consuming frozen amount if needed); burnFrom: any allowance holder, sender policy and unfrozen balance required <br>_rule_ | Constant | `burn: require_role(ISSUER_ROLE, actor); burn_from: allowance + require_can_send(from) + require_unfrozen` | [`crates/core/stablecoin/src/state.rs:570`](../crates/core/stablecoin/src/state.rs#L570) | Two burn paths; both require the token to be unpaused and a non-zero amount.<br>_Detail:_ ISSUER burn gate at state.rs:550. |
| Pause / unpause | GUARDIAN pauses; ADMIN unpauses <br>_rule_ | Constant | `pause: require_role(GUARDIAN_ROLE); unpause: require_role(ADMIN_ROLE)` | [`crates/core/stablecoin/src/state.rs:275`](../crates/core/stablecoin/src/state.rs#L275) | While paused, transfers, mints, burns and forced transfers revert and canSend/canReceive/canTransfer return false.<br>_Detail:_ Unpause gate at state.rs:287. |

Allowance rule at state.rs:829. |
| Forced transfer | ENFORCER; consumes frozen amount as needed; no self-transfer; recipient must pass receive policy <br>_rule_ | Constant | `require_role(ENFORCER_ROLE); if from == to { ForcedSelfTransfer }; require_can_receive(to)` | [`crates/core/stablecoin/src/state.rs:616`](../crates/core/stablecoin/src/state.rs#L616) | Moves tokens without the holder's consent; the sender's send policy is not checked and the frozen amount is reduced by whatever exceeds the unfrozen balance. |

## Oracle, price feeds & bridges

COEN and currency price formation (VWAP), the off-chain feeder, the emit note tree, Hyperlane and L2 registries.

Scope: `crates/system/{oracle, offchain-data, emit, hyperlanecontroller, l2registry, radicleregistry} · bin/{outbe-feeder, outbe-hyperlane-attester}`

Begin-block tallies all pending votes when block_number is a non-zero multiple of vote_period; each validator (or its delegated feeder) may submit one vote per period.<br>_Detail:_ Testnet sets 8 (scripts/testnet.yaml:149; also scripts/network.example.yaml). Tally trigger at crates/system/oracle/src/lifecycle.rs:77. Zero is rejected at genesis. The feeder default is also 2 (bin/outbe-feeder/src/config.rs:124) and the feeder refuses to vote when its configured period differs from on-chain getParams. |
| ★ Per-pair validator quorum | ceil(2N/3) of active validators (N - floor(N/3)) <br>_validators_ | Derived | `fn pair_quorum(active_validator_count: usize) -> usize { active_validator_count - active_validator_count / 3 }` | [`crates/system/oracle/src/tally.rs:328`](../crates/system/oracle/src/tally.rs#L328) | A pair is tallied only when at least this many active validators submitted a positive rate for it; every active validator counts once regardless of stake. Membership is re-read at tally time, so votes from validators that left the active set are ignored.<br>_Detail:_ Applied per pair at tally.rs:446. A pair below quorum keeps its previous stored rate (no update, no snapshot); validators with a valid observation on that pair still receive participation credit (tally.rs:452-462). |
| ★ Validator price aggregation (median) | Unweighted median of positive validator rates; even count -> floored midpoint <br>_rule_ | Derived | `lower + (upper - lower) / U256::from(2u64)` | [`crates/system/oracle/src/tally.rs:106`](../crates/system/oracle/src/tally.rs#L106) | Each validator's rate counts once (no stake weighting). Zero-rate rows mark participation but are excluded from the median, deviation and winner calculation.<br>_Detail:_ There is no per-update maximum price change, circuit breaker or deviation-from-previous-rate bound on-chain; the new median overwrites the stored rate directly. The only input bounds are the absolute vote caps (MAX_VOTE_PRICE_WHOLE / MAX_VOTE_VOLUME_WHOLE). |
| ★ Reward band | 2% (0.02 x 1e18) <br>_FP18 ratio_ | Genesis | `reward_band: U256::from(20_000_000_000_000_000u128), // 0.02` | [`crates/system/oracle/src/genesis.rs:105`](../crates/system/oracle/src/genesis.rs#L105) | Width parameter of the winner band: a vote wins a pair when it lies within median +/- max(sigma, median x reward_band / 2), i.e. at least +/-1% of the median by default.<br>_Detail:_ The oracle module pays no feeder rewards; winning only decides success vs miss counters used by the slash window. Testnet uses the same value (scripts/testnet.yaml:150). |
| Reward spread (winner bound) | max(population std-dev of rates, median x reward_band / 2) <br>_formula_ | Derived | `reward_spread = max(std_dev, median * reward_band / (2 * FP18))` | [`crates/system/oracle/src/tally.rs:201`](../crates/system/oracle/src/tally.rs#L201) | Standard deviation is the integer population sigma of positive rates around the median (Newton integer sqrt); votes in [median - spread, median + spread] are winners. |
| Tallied volume per pair | Median of the reward-band winners' volumes <br>_rule_ | Derived | `volume: sorted_median(volumes.len(), \|index\| volumes[index])` | [`crates/system/oracle/src/tally.rs:228`](../crates/system/oracle/src/tally.rs#L228) | The volume written with each snapshot entry is the (floored-midpoint) median of the winning validators' reported volumes, so a single outlier volume does not dominate VWAP weights. |

| Per-round success / abstain / miss | success only if the validator wins on every vote target <br>_rule_ | Derived | `if claim.win_count == total_targets { increment_success } else if !claim.did_vote { increment_abstain } else { increment_miss }` | [`crates/system/oracle/src/tally.rs:565`](../crates/system/oracle/src/tally.rs#L565) | After each tally every active validator gets one counter increment: success (winner or quorum-less credit on all targets), abstain (no submission at all) or miss (submitted, including zero-rate rows, but not winning everywhere).<br>_Detail:_ If nobody voted in a period, every active validator gets an abstain (tally.rs:379-385). |
| Max price per vote tuple | 1,000,000 whole units (raw 1e12 on COEN/ISO) <br>_whole quote units per base unit_ | Constant | `pub(crate) const MAX_VOTE_PRICE_WHOLE: u64 = 1_000_000;` | [`crates/system/oracle/src/constants.rs:50`](../crates/system/oracle/src/constants.rs#L50) | submitVote rejects a tuple whose rate exceeds 1,000,000 x pair scale; the check runs before the voted flag is set, so the feeder can resubmit in the same period.<br>_Detail:_ Absolute ceiling only (reject_vote_outside_market_bounds at crates/system/oracle/src/runtime.rs:202-229, called at :170-172); not a deviation bound. |
| Max volume per vote tuple | 1,000,000,000,000 whole units (raw 1e18 on COEN/ISO) <br>_whole base units_ | Constant | `pub(crate) const MAX_VOTE_VOLUME_WHOLE: u64 = 1_000_000_000_000;` | [`crates/system/oracle/src/constants.rs:54`](../crates/system/oracle/src/constants.rs#L54) | submitVote rejects a tuple whose volume exceeds 1e12 x pair scale. |

### Oracle / S-curve

| Parameter | Value | How set | Code | Source | What it controls |

| ★ S-curve coefficient table | 1.000000 (day 0) -> 0.959980 (day 64) -> 0.920000 (day 127) <br>_scale 1e6_ | Constant | `pub static COEFFICIENTS: [U256; PERIOD] = [1_000_000, 999_960, ..., 920_000]` | [`crates/system/oracle/src/scurve.rs:48`](../crates/system/oracle/src/scurve.rs#L48) | Value on day d after a peak = anchor x COEFFICIENTS[d mod 128], floor-rounded at 1e6; coefficients decrease monotonically.<br>_Detail:_ Each value is floor(original decimal18 / 1e12) from the Cosmos x/oracle precomputed table; pinned by testdata/coen840-scurve-v1.json. |
| S-curve shape parameters | alpha = 0.08, beta = 0.08, center = day 64 <br>_dimensionless_ | Constant | `coef_i = 1 - alpha * (sigmoid(x_i) - sigmoid_min) / (sigmoid_0 - sigmoid_min); alpha=0.08, beta=0.08, center=64` | [`crates/system/oracle/src/scurve.rs:43`](../crates/system/oracle/src/scurve.rs#L43) | Generator parameters documented for the precomputed coefficient table (the runtime uses the stored table, not the formula). |

| S-curve peak detection rule | close(D-3) < close(D-2) > close(D-1) <br>_rule_ | Derived | `if close_d3 < close_d2 && close_d2 > close_d1 && store_scurve_entry_inner(oracle, pair, day_minus_2, close_d2)?` | [`crates/system/oracle/src/scurve.rs:448`](../crates/system/oracle/src/scurve.rs#L448) | At the first block of each UTC day, for each registered active COEN/<reference> pair, the last snapshot rate of each of the three prior closed days is compared; a confirmed peak at D-2 becomes the pair's S-curve anchor.<br>_Detail:_ Daily hook at crates/system/oracle/src/lifecycle.rs:84-92. A new peak replaces the pair's single chain only if its price exceeds the current curve value on that day (scurve.rs:372). |

CEX: binance, kraken, okx, gate, huobi, mexc, coinbase (WebSocket + REST); DEX: uniswap, pancakeswap; aggregators: pyth (Hermes), chainlink (CryptoCompare REST); test: mock, mock_http.<br>_Detail:_ The 'chainlink' provider actually queries CryptoCompare (bin/outbe-feeder/src/provider/chainlink.rs:14). |

The node-side zero-fee policy caps a gasless vote at 16 KiB calldata and 1,500,000 gas (crates/system/zerofee/src/oracle.rs:14-17, outside this scope). |

### Price feeder / Scheduling & submission

| Parameter | Value | How set | Code | Source | What it controls |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Aggregation timeout | 15 s <br>_seconds_ | Constant | `tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(15), aggregator::fetch_and_aggregate(providers, config))` | [`bin/outbe-feeder/src/main.rs:286`](../bin/outbe-feeder/src/main.rs#L286) | Upper bound on one fetch-and-aggregate pass across all providers; on timeout the tick fails and retries on the next poll without consuming the period. |
| Oracle freshness read timeout | 2 s <br>_seconds_ | Constant | `tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(2), monitor)` | [`bin/outbe-feeder/src/main.rs:228`](../bin/outbe-feeder/src/main.rs#L228) | Bound on the per-tick read of each pair's on-chain lastBlock/lastTimestamp for health; voting continues if it times out. |
| Stalled vote replacement threshold | > max(2 x vote_period, 8) blocks since signing <br>_blocks_ | Constant | `head.height.saturating_sub(pending.observed_height) > config.oracle.vote_period.saturating_mul(2).max(8)` | [`bin/outbe-feeder/src/main.rs:261`](../bin/outbe-feeder/src/main.rs#L261) | A journaled vote not mined within this many blocks (or reverted) is re-signed at the same nonce with a higher fee instead of queuing a new nonce. |
| Feeder RPC timeout | 5 s <br>_seconds_ | Constant | `reqwest::Client::builder().timeout(Duration::from_secs(5))` | [`bin/outbe-feeder/src/oracle_client.rs:74`](../bin/outbe-feeder/src/oracle_client.rs#L74) | HTTP timeout for every JSON-RPC call to the Outbe node; a timeout is treated as an unknown outcome, never as permission to allocate a new nonce. |
| Vote transaction gas limit | 1,000,000 gas <br>_gas_ | Constant | `.gas_limit(1_000_000)` | [`bin/outbe-feeder/src/oracle_client.rs:279`](../bin/outbe-feeder/src/oracle_client.rs#L279) | Fixed gas limit on every signed submitVote transaction.<br>_Detail:_ Below the zero-fee oracle cap of 1,500,000 gas (crates/system/zerofee/src/oracle.rs:17, outside scope). |
| Vote max fee per gas | 2 x max(eth_gasPrice, MIN_PROTOCOL_BASE_FEE) <br>_wei per gas_ | Constant | `let mut cap = gas_price.max(MIN_PROTOCOL_BASE_FEE as u128).saturating_mul(2);` | [`bin/outbe-feeder/src/oracle_client.rs:262`](../bin/outbe-feeder/src/oracle_client.rs#L262) | EIP-1559 max fee for a new vote transaction.<br>_Detail:_ MIN_PROTOCOL_BASE_FEE is alloy's EIP-1559 minimum base fee (7 wei upstream; the alloy source is not vendored here, so not verified locally). |
| Replacement fee bump | new max fee >= old + old/4 + 1 (about +25%) <br>_wei per gas_ | Constant | `cap = cap.max(old.max_fee_per_gas.saturating_add(old.max_fee_per_gas / 4).saturating_add(1))` | [`bin/outbe-feeder/src/oracle_client.rs:266`](../bin/outbe-feeder/src/oracle_client.rs#L266) | Same-nonce replacement transactions raise the max fee by at least 25% plus one wei over the journaled transaction. |
| Vote priority fee | 0 when gasless; otherwise max(eth_gasPrice, previous max fee) <br>_wei per gas_ | Constant | `let priority = if chain.gasless_oracle_votes { 0 } else { gas_price.max(replacement.map(\|old\| old.max_fee_per_gas).unwrap_or(0)) };` | [`bin/outbe-feeder/src/oracle_client.rs:270`](../bin/outbe-feeder/src/oracle_client.rs#L270) | Priority fee attached to vote transactions. |
| Preflight validator status requirement | status == 2 (Active); signer resolved for role 1 (Oracle) <br>_enum value_ | Constant | `if status.status != 2 { Blocked("validator is not Active") }` | [`bin/outbe-feeder/src/oracle_client.rs:168`](../bin/outbe-feeder/src/oracle_client.rs#L168) | Before aggregating, the feeder checks oracle enabled, period match, Active validator status, signer delegation and that no vote exists yet in the period. |
| Feeder health staleness timeout | max(vote_period x 60 s, 60 s) <br>_seconds_ | Constant | `Duration::from_secs(self.vote_period.saturating_mul(60).max(60))` | [`bin/outbe-feeder/src/health.rs:147`](../bin/outbe-feeder/src/health.rs#L147) | /health turns unhealthy when the last confirmed vote, head observation, head progress, pair observation or pending transaction is older than this (also the startup grace). |
| Feeder pair-update staleness | > 5 x vote_period blocks behind head <br>_blocks_ | Constant | `head.height - oracle.block > self.vote_period.saturating_mul(5)` | [`bin/outbe-feeder/src/health.rs:190`](../bin/outbe-feeder/src/health.rs#L190) | /health reports 'last update is stale' when a pair's on-chain rate block lags the observed head by more than five vote periods. |

| Parameter | Value | How set | Code | Source | What it controls |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Per-source observation | Candle TVWAP when candles exist, else ticker price/volume <br>_rule_ | Derived | `if let Some(candles) = candles { compute_weighted(&values) } else { ticker }` | [`bin/outbe-feeder/src/aggregator.rs:210`](../bin/outbe-feeder/src/aggregator.rs#L210) | Each configured provider market yields one observation: the volume-weighted mean of its recent candle closes (equal-duration candles) when available, otherwise its latest ticker. |
| ★ Feeder pair aggregation | Volume-weighted mean of filtered source observations, floored; zero volume weighted as 1 unit <br>_formula_ | Derived | `let weight = if volume.is_zero() { SCALE_1E18 } else { volume };` | [`bin/outbe-feeder/src/aggregator.rs:72`](../bin/outbe-feeder/src/aggregator.rs#L72) | All sources nested under one currency_pairs entry are deviation-filtered and combined into one vote tuple; the published volume is the sum of source volumes (zero-volume sources contribute weight 1 but volume 0).<br>_Detail:_ compute_weighted at aggregator.rs:61. DEX v1 assumes USDC = USDT = 1 USD (no stablecoin/USD correction). |
| Deviation filter formula | keep \|p - median\| <= threshold x sigma; median = sorted[n/2]; sigma = population std-dev around the mean <br>_formula_ | Derived | `let allowed = U512::from(threshold.raw()) * U512::from(std_dev) / U512::from(SCALE_1E18);` | [`bin/outbe-feeder/src/aggregator.rs:275`](../bin/outbe-feeder/src/aggregator.rs#L275) | Applied only when there are at least two observations and sigma > 0; uses the upper median for even counts and integer arithmetic throughout.<br>_Detail:_ filter_deviations at aggregator.rs:245; median at :255. |
| Feeder wire scale conversion | FP18 / 1e12 -> 1e6 for COEN/ISO; sub-minor price dropped; positive sub-minor volume -> 1 minor unit <br>_scale_ | Constant | `const SCALE_1E12: U256 = ...1_000_000_000_000...; let price = price / SCALE_1E12;` | [`bin/outbe-feeder/src/aggregator.rs:43`](../bin/outbe-feeder/src/aggregator.rs#L43) | Provider decimals are parsed into deterministic FP18 integers; COEN/ISO tuples are floored to 6 decimals, generic pairs stay at 18.<br>_Detail:_ SCALE_1E12 at aggregator.rs:38. Decimal parsing truncates digits beyond 18 decimals (bin/outbe-feeder/src/fixed.rs:36-95). |
| Provider fetch timeout | 5 s for tickers + 5 s for candles, per provider <br>_seconds_ | Constant | `tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5), provider.get_ticker_prices(&source_pairs))` | [`bin/outbe-feeder/src/aggregator.rs:123`](../bin/outbe-feeder/src/aggregator.rs#L123) | A provider that errors or times out is logged and skipped; no fallback price is fabricated.<br>_Detail:_ Candle timeout at aggregator.rs:144. Individual REST requests also use a 5 s HTTP timeout (e.g. bin/outbe-feeder/src/provider/binance.rs:69). |

## TEE attestation & OCOMP

Enclave attestation and registry rules, and the off-chain computation protocol whose results validators vote on.

Scope: `crates/system/{tee, teeregistry, ocomp-protocol, ocompregistry} · bin/{outbe-tee-enclave, outbe-ocomp} · release/`

### TEE attestation

| Parameter | Value | How set | Code | Source | What it controls |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

## Consensus, engine & operator

Block production cadence, consensus timeouts and quorums, finality proofs, sync and the operator service.

Scope: `crates/blockchain/{consensus, engine, operator, snapshot, radicle, offchain-storage}`

### Consensus protocol

| Parameter | Value | How set | Code | Source | What it controls |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| ★ Consensus algorithm | Commonware Simplex BFT with Outbe HybridScheme signatures (execute-then-agree) <br>_-_ | Constant | `simplex::Engine::new(.., simplex::Config { scheme: HybridScheme::<MinSig>, elector: HybridRandom, .. })` | [`crates/blockchain/engine/src/stack/epoch/simplex.rs:191`](../crates/blockchain/engine/src/stack/epoch/simplex.rs#L191) | Each consensus epoch runs one Commonware Simplex engine. Votes are individual BLS12-381 MinPk signatures that are aggregated into a single certificate with a signer bitmap, and each vote also carries a BLS12-381 MinSig threshold seed partial used as the VRF for leader selection. Every proposed block is a fully executed, sealed EVM block that validators re-execute before voting.<br>_Detail:_ commonware-* crates pinned to tag v2026.9.0 (workspace Cargo.toml:249-260). HybridScheme lives in crates/blockchain/consensus/src/hybrid.rs; its doc (hybrid.rs:16) states a ~162-byte certificate for any committee size. Execute-then-agree model documented in crates/blockchain/consensus/src/timing.rs:12-26. |
| Simplex engine policy flags | forward = Disabled, skip = Disabled, track_historical_votes = true, strategy = Sequential <br>_-_ | Constant | `forward: simplex::ForwardPolicy::Disabled, skip: SkipPolicy::Disabled, track_historical_votes: true, strategy: commonware_parallel::Sequential` | [`crates/blockchain/engine/src/stack/epoch/simplex.rs:197`](../crates/blockchain/engine/src/stack/epoch/simplex.rs#L197) | Fixed engine settings passed to every per-epoch Simplex instance: certificate forwarding and view skipping are disabled, historical votes are tracked, and verification runs sequentially (the HybridScheme also reports is_batchable() = false).<br>_Detail:_ Lines 197 (forward), 199 (strategy), 211 (skip), 212 (track_historical_votes). Semantics of the commonware policy enums are defined upstream and could not be inspected offline. |

| Parameter | Value | How set | Code | Source | What it controls |

View 1 of an epoch > 0 uses the previous epoch's last finalized VRF seed as bootstrap seed (crates/blockchain/engine/src/stack/epoch/simplex.rs:12-38). |
| Round-robin leader fallback | leader index = (epoch + view) mod n <br>_-_ | Constant | `Participant::new((round.epoch().get().wrapping_add(round.view().get())) as u32 % self.n)` | [`crates/blockchain/consensus/src/hybrid/election.rs:190`](../crates/blockchain/consensus/src/hybrid/election.rs#L190) | Fallback used when no verified, bootstrap or degraded seed is available - in practice the first view after chain genesis, so VRF-based election is active from view 2. If a certificate's VRF proof fails verification, a deterministic degraded seed (bootstrap seed \|\| round) is used instead and a metric is emitted.<br>_Detail:_ Start-up log 'VRF active from view 2' at crates/blockchain/engine/src/stack/epoch/run.rs:470; degraded path at election.rs:213-232. |

Follower certificate decoding uses a literal 256 rather than this constant (crates/blockchain/consensus/src/follow/upstream.rs:63, crates/blockchain/engine/src/stack/follower.rs:54). |
| Committee membership by validator status | voting = ACTIVE; consensus participants = ACTIVE + EXITING; reshare target = ACTIVE + PENDING <br>_-_ | Constant | `read_validators_from_state / read_consensus_validators_from_state / read_reshare_target_from_state` | [`crates/blockchain/engine/src/validators.rs:87`](../crates/blockchain/engine/src/validators.rs#L87) | Validator sets are read from the ValidatorSet precompile at specific heights: Simplex start/restart uses ACTIVE and EXITING validators that still hold BLS shares, while the DKG reshare target (next players) is ACTIVE plus PENDING joiners, frozen at the DKG freeze height. EXITING validators are removed by the next reshare.<br>_Detail:_ ACTIVE-only reader at validators.rs:78; reshare target reader at validators.rs:98. A removed validator holding a previous share acts as dealer-only in the reshare. A committee member without a threshold share runs the Simplex engine in verifier mode (follows and verifies, cannot propose or vote) until it receives a share at the next reshare (crates/blockchain/engine/src/stack/epoch/simplex.rs:81-105). |

Genesis tooling can deal centrally; live networks run the interactive DKG ceremony.<br>_Detail:_ Doc at bls.rs:464-465 ('requires 2f+1 of 3f+1 participants'). ### Epochs & DKG

| Parameter | Value | How set | Code | Source | What it controls |

| ★ Epoch length | 1,200 blocks (default) <br>_blocks_ | Genesis | `pub const DEFAULT_EPOCH_LENGTH_BLOCKS: u32 = 1_200;  // genesis config.epochLengthBlocks` | [`crates/blockchain/consensus/src/config.rs:84`](../crates/blockchain/consensus/src/config.rs#L84) | Cadence of DKG reshare, active-set rotation and the per-epoch slash-counter reset: the next planned activation is the last activation height + epoch length, and each DKG activation advances the consensus epoch by one and restarts Simplex with the new committee. Read from genesis config.epochLengthBlocks (must be > 0) with this default.<br>_Detail:_ Reader: crates/blockchain/engine/src/stack/startup.rs:5-26 (deprecated keys epochDuration / dkgRotationIntervalBlocks are rejected). Comment says '~1 hour at a ~3s block'; at the 2 s minimum block time 1,200 blocks is >= 40 min. Network generators default to 300 (scripts/create_genesis.py:76, scripts/prepare_network.py:69; E2E fixture genesis uses 300) while scripts/seed_genesis.py:237 uses 1,200. The value is also seeded into ValidatorSet storage (config_epoch_length_blocks, slot 2) read by the EVM - two copies of one genesis value. Validator marshal maps heights with FixedEpocher(epochLengthBlocks) (crates/blockchain/engine/src/stack/epoch/marshal_recovery.rs:102) whereas the follower uses FollowerEpocher, whose docs state a fixed (height-1)/L mapping is not authoritative after a grace-delayed activation - worth reviewing. |
| ★ DKG prepare window | 600 blocks (default; clamped to <= epoch length) <br>_blocks_ | Genesis | `pub const DEFAULT_DKG_PREPARE_WINDOW_BLOCKS: u64 = 600;  // genesis config.dkgPrepareWindowBlocks` | [`crates/blockchain/consensus/src/config.rs:90`](../crates/blockchain/consensus/src/config.rs#L90) | Number of blocks before the planned activation at which the validator set is frozen and the reshare DKG starts (freeze height = planned activation - prepare window). Read from genesis config.dkgPrepareWindowBlocks and clamped to the epoch length.<br>_Detail:_ Reader: crates/blockchain/engine/src/stack/dkg/handoff.rs:13-16, clamp at :25. Malformed genesis values silently fall back to the default (.and_then(\|r\| r.ok())), unlike epochLengthBlocks which errors. Comment says '~10 minutes at ~1s/block' but blocks are >= 2 s, so 600 blocks is >= 20 min. config.rs:82 refers to 'config.dkg{Prepare,Grace}*' while the real keys are dkgPrepareWindowBlocks/dkgActivationGraceBlocks. Generators default to 30 (scripts/create_genesis.py:77, scripts/prepare_network.py:70). |
| ★ DKG activation grace window | 600 blocks (default) <br>_blocks_ | Genesis | `pub const DEFAULT_DKG_ACTIVATION_GRACE_BLOCKS: u64 = 600;  // genesis config.dkgActivationGraceBlocks` | [`crates/blockchain/consensus/src/config.rs:92`](../crates/blockchain/consensus/src/config.rs#L92) | How many blocks after the planned activation height a completed reshare may still activate (the activation anchor is max(planned activation, finalized pre-announce carrier)). Past planned activation + grace the VRF material is considered expired and proposing stops. Read from genesis config.dkgActivationGraceBlocks.<br>_Detail:_ Reader: crates/blockchain/engine/src/stack/dkg/handoff.rs:17-20 (silent fallback on malformed values; not clamped). Also bounds the follower's accepted epoch-boundary window. Generators default to 30 (scripts/create_genesis.py:78, scripts/prepare_network.py:71). |

Chain spec, gas and fee rules, precompile gas, system transactions, txpool and RPC limits, and node defaults.

Scope: `crates/blockchain/{evm, primitives, node, txpool, rpc, macros} · bin/{outbe-chain, outbe-cli, outbe-keygen}`

### Gas & fees

| Parameter | Value | How set | Code | Source | What it controls |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| ★ Base fee (EIP-1559) parameters | Ethereum defaults inherited (elasticity 2, max change 1/8 per block) <br>_base-fee rule_ | Constant | `validate_against_parent_eip1559_base_fee(header, parent, self.chain_spec.as_ref()); chain_spec().next_block_base_fee(parent, timestamp)` | [`crates/blockchain/node/src/consensus.rs:175`](../crates/blockchain/node/src/consensus.rs#L175) | Next-block base fee and its validation use the reth ChainSpec base-fee parameters; Outbe defines no custom elasticity, denominator or minimum base fee.<br>⚠ No override found anywhere in scope; the numeric values are upstream reth/alloy defaults for a ChainSpec built from genesis (BaseFeeParams::ethereum()), not verifiable from vendored source here. Initial base fee comes from genesis baseFeePerGas (0x7 in release/testnet-genesis.json; not set by scripts/create_genesis.py). Note storage/gas.rs:3 says Outbe 'does not use a fee market', which conflicts with the active EIP-1559 base fee + tip path. |

## Solidity contracts, genesis & network config

Constants in the Solidity contracts (Intex, intents, tokens, cross-chain) and the values that define the testnet genesis and network tooling.

Scope: `contracts/ · release/testnet-genesis.json · scripts/ · deploy/`

## Inconsistencies and caveats

Where code and canon (`scripts/canon/canon.md`), or two parts of the repository, disagree. Each was checked against the cited lines.

1. **Call price is 2.28× for Intex and Gem, not 1.64×.** The canon sets the Call Price at entry × 1.64 for Intex and Gem. The code uses CALL_RATE = 128 (2.28×) for Intex and Gem and CALL_RATE_PCT = 256 (3.56×) for Nod. 1.64× appears only as the Credis call rate, applied to the previous day's COEN price at opening.  
   Sources: [`crates/core/intexfactory/src/constants.rs:57`](../crates/core/intexfactory/src/constants.rs#L57), [`crates/core/gem/src/constants.rs:44`](../crates/core/gem/src/constants.rs#L44), [`crates/core/nod/src/constants.rs:25`](../crates/core/nod/src/constants.rs#L25), [`crates/core/credis/src/constants.rs:16`](../crates/core/credis/src/constants.rs#L16), [`scripts/canon/canon.md:118`](../scripts/canon/canon.md#L118)
2. **Call trigger counts 21 of 28 days, not 21 of 30.** The canon calls a contract when the price is above its Call Price on 21 of 30 days. Nod, Intex, Gem and Credis all scan the last 28 closed UTC days with a 21-day threshold.  
   Sources: [`crates/core/intexfactory/src/constants.rs:63`](../crates/core/intexfactory/src/constants.rs#L63), [`crates/core/gem/src/constants.rs:24`](../crates/core/gem/src/constants.rs#L24), [`crates/core/nod/src/constants.rs:35`](../crates/core/nod/src/constants.rs#L35), [`crates/core/credis/src/constants.rs:26`](../crates/core/credis/src/constants.rs#L26), [`scripts/canon/canon.md:118`](../scripts/canon/canon.md#L118)
3. **Nods are called and forfeited.** The canon applies Call to Intex and Gem only and gives Nod no expiry. The code seals call terms on every Nod bucket, calls it on the same 21-of-28 rule, and forfeit-burns unpaid Nods after the 7-day notice.  
   Sources: [`crates/core/nod/src/constants.rs:25`](../crates/core/nod/src/constants.rs#L25), [`crates/core/nod/src/constants.rs:54`](../crates/core/nod/src/constants.rs#L54), [`scripts/canon/canon.md:100`](../scripts/canon/canon.md#L100), [`scripts/canon/canon.md:118`](../scripts/canon/canon.md#L118)
4. **No 21-day wait before settlement.** The canon requires 21 days elapsed and a COEN/ref price above the Floor Price. In code a right qualifies once any finalized UTC-day VWAP since its first full day closes above the floor, and the settle paths add no time gate. The 502-hour Metadosis lookback delay runs before Tributes are offered, not between issuance and settlement.  
   Sources: [`crates/core/nod/src/api.rs:37`](../crates/core/nod/src/api.rs#L37), [`crates/system/oracle/src/api.rs:463`](../crates/system/oracle/src/api.rs#L463), [`crates/core/nodfactory/src/runtime.rs:210`](../crates/core/nodfactory/src/runtime.rs#L210), [`scripts/canon/canon.md:116`](../scripts/canon/canon.md#L116)
5. **Cost Amount is priced in the reference currency.** The canon states entry price and Cost Amount in the issuance currency. The code stores the entry price and computes the cost in the reference currency; paying in the issuance currency converts at a VWAP-snapshot cross rate.  
   Sources: [`crates/core/nod/src/api.rs:80`](../crates/core/nod/src/api.rs#L80), [`crates/core/nodfactory/src/runtime.rs:447`](../crates/core/nodfactory/src/runtime.rs#L447), [`scripts/canon/canon.md:110`](../scripts/canon/canon.md#L110), [`scripts/canon/canon.md:112`](../scripts/canon/canon.md#L112)
6. **Merchant Gems copy currencies and floor the entry price.** The canon has a Gem inherit only the parent's entry price, with merchant-elected currencies. The Gem Factory copies both currencies from the source Intex series and sets the entry price to the higher of the previous day's COEN price and the parent's entry price.  
   Sources: [`crates/core/gemfactory/src/runtime.rs:164`](../crates/core/gemfactory/src/runtime.rs#L164), [`crates/core/gemfactory/src/runtime.rs:258`](../crates/core/gemfactory/src/runtime.rs#L258), [`scripts/canon/canon.md:104`](../scripts/canon/canon.md#L104)
7. **Gems are also issued without a parent Intex.** Besides Merchant Gems, the Gem Factory issues Genesis, Validator, SRA, Wallet and CCA Gems directly, for validator and agent rewards. The canon describes a Gem only as a merchant fragmentation of an Intex.  
   Sources: [`crates/core/gemfactory/src/schema.rs:11`](../crates/core/gemfactory/src/schema.rs#L11), [`scripts/canon/canon.md:104`](../scripts/canon/canon.md#L104)
8. **The emission limit rises before it decays.** The canon describes a fixed daily budget decaying over time. The code's curve climbs from 2²⁸ COEN on day 0 to about 581.6M on day 1,024, then falls to the 2²⁶ floor on day 3,072.  
   Sources: [`crates/system/emissionlimit/src/day_emission.rs:3`](../crates/system/emissionlimit/src/day_emission.rs#L3), [`scripts/canon/canon.md:138`](../scripts/canon/canon.md#L138)
9. **Mainnet genesis tooling starts from the testnet profile.** create_genesis.py uses scripts/testnet.yaml as the baseline for every network and, for mainnet, only rejects protocol_constants. Sections that mainnet.example.yaml leaves out carry over: the DEV IntexFactory profile, demo balances and a demo Gem, and oracle penalties set to 0.  
   Sources: [`scripts/create_genesis.py:61`](../scripts/create_genesis.py#L61), [`scripts/create_genesis.py:389`](../scripts/create_genesis.py#L389), [`scripts/testnet.yaml:181`](../scripts/testnet.yaml#L181), [`scripts/testnet.yaml:153`](../scripts/testnet.yaml#L153), [`scripts/mainnet.example.yaml:30`](../scripts/mainnet.example.yaml#L30)
10. **Test networks run short DEV terms by default.** Nod, Intex and Gem pick their profile by chain id unless genesis sets one, and only chain 676 runs PROD. Elsewhere the call price is 1.10× entry on 2 of 3 days with a 3-day notice, and Intex and Gem floors are 1.05×.  
   Sources: [`crates/core/nod/src/config.rs:41`](../crates/core/nod/src/config.rs#L41), [`crates/core/gem/src/config.rs:47`](../crates/core/gem/src/config.rs#L47), [`crates/core/intexfactory/src/config.rs:47`](../crates/core/intexfactory/src/config.rs#L47)
11. **Block reward split in the config is never read.** testnet.yaml and the seed files declare proposer 50% / staking 30% / treasury 20%. No Rust code or seeding step reads these keys; block fees are shared equally among committee members.  
   Sources: [`scripts/testnet.yaml:132`](../scripts/testnet.yaml#L132), [`crates/system/rewards/src/constants.rs:60`](../crates/system/rewards/src/constants.rs#L60)
12. **Epoch length defaults disagree.** create_genesis.py defaults to 300-block epochs, while seed_genesis.py and the consensus layer fall back to 1,200. At 300 blocks the 500-missed-vote voter felony threshold is larger than the epoch itself.  
   Sources: [`scripts/create_genesis.py:76`](../scripts/create_genesis.py#L76), [`scripts/seed_genesis.py:237`](../scripts/seed_genesis.py#L237), [`crates/system/slashindicator/src/runtime.rs:52`](../crates/system/slashindicator/src/runtime.rs#L52)
13. **OCOMP vote-window override is never read.** config.outbeProtocol ocomp.computeVoteWindowBlocks is validated at startup, but its getter is only called in tests. Production checks the OCOMP profile against the compiled 1,800-block default.  
   Sources: [`crates/blockchain/constants/src/lib.rs:452`](../crates/blockchain/constants/src/lib.rs#L452), [`crates/system/ocompregistry/src/profile.rs:86`](../crates/system/ocompregistry/src/profile.rs#L86)
14. **A documented override key would be rejected.** network.example.yaml and testnet.yaml list metadosis.bootstrapDurationSeconds among the override knobs. The override parser denies unknown fields and has no such field, so a genesis that copies it fails to load.  
   Sources: [`scripts/network.example.yaml:124`](../scripts/network.example.yaml#L124), [`scripts/testnet.yaml:114`](../scripts/testnet.yaml#L114), [`crates/blockchain/constants/src/lib.rs:156`](../crates/blockchain/constants/src/lib.rs#L156)
15. **Day windows start at midnight UTC+14, not UTC.** network.example.yaml says a worldwide day measures its windows from midnight UTC. The code starts the forming window at 00:00 UTC+14, which is 10:00 UTC on the previous date.  
   Sources: [`scripts/network.example.yaml:103`](../scripts/network.example.yaml#L103), [`crates/blockchain/primitives/src/time.rs:189`](../crates/blockchain/primitives/src/time.rs#L189)
16. **Credis comments disagree with the constants.** constants.rs describes the call price as entry + 64%, but the markup applies to the call anchor price. called.rs says called positions lapse 14 days later; the notice period is 7 days.  
   Sources: [`crates/core/credis/src/constants.rs:15`](../crates/core/credis/src/constants.rs#L15), [`crates/core/credisfactory/src/called.rs:48`](../crates/core/credisfactory/src/called.rs#L48), [`crates/core/credis/src/constants.rs:46`](../crates/core/credis/src/constants.rs#L46)
17. **Hyperlane liveness cadence comment.** runtime.rs calls the 150-block liveness window the same cadence as the oracle slash window, which defaults to 96 blocks.  
   Sources: [`crates/system/hyperlanecontroller/src/runtime.rs:21`](../crates/system/hyperlanecontroller/src/runtime.rs#L21), [`crates/system/oracle/src/genesis.rs:106`](../crates/system/oracle/src/genesis.rs#L106)

## Fixed currencies

Places where a currency is hard-coded, mostly USD (ISO 840). _Protocol rule_ entries decide outcomes for every currency.

### Protocol rules fixed to USD (consensus code)

| What | Kind | Where | Change / note |
|---|---|---|---|
| Day colour (GREEN / RED) is decided on COEN/USD only | Protocol rule | [`crates/system/oracle/src/constants.rs:32`](../crates/system/oracle/src/constants.rs#L32) | Decide whether another currency or a basket should count; today a day is GREEN only if the COEN/USD WorldwideDay VWAP rose.<br>DAY_TYPE_ISO = 840. Every RED day divides the Nod side by 8 and zeroes the Intex side, for all currencies. |
| Promis load per Intex is anchored to COEN/USD | Protocol rule | [`crates/core/desis/src/constants.rs:7`](../crates/core/desis/src/constants.rs#L7) | Ladder steps by decades of the COEN/USD rate.<br>PROMIS_LOAD_ANCHOR_ISO = 840, a copy of DAY_TYPE_ISO under another name. |
| Agent reward Gems (WAA, SRA, CCA) issued and referenced in USD | Protocol rule | [`crates/core/agentreward/src/runtime.rs:12`](../crates/core/agentreward/src/runtime.rs#L12) | CR-003 proposes agent-chosen currencies.<br>AGENT_GEM_CURRENCY = 840; entry price is the previous day's COEN/USD VWAP. |
| Validator reward Gems issued and referenced in USD | Protocol rule | [`crates/system/rewards/src/constants.rs:17`](../crates/system/rewards/src/constants.rs#L17) | Same decision as CR-003, for validators.<br>REWARD_GEM_CURRENCY = 840. |
| USD must be one of the reference currencies | Protocol rule | [`crates/system/oracle/src/openings.rs:28`](../crates/system/oracle/src/openings.rs#L28)<br>[`crates/system/ocomp-protocol/src/input.rs:330`](../crates/system/ocomp-protocol/src/input.rs#L330)<br>[`crates/system/ocomp-protocol/src/opening.rs:112`](../crates/system/ocomp-protocol/src/opening.rs#L112)<br>[`crates/system/ocomp-protocol/src/opening.rs:172`](../crates/system/ocomp-protocol/src/opening.rs#L172)<br>[`crates/core/nod/src/openings.rs:18`](../crates/core/nod/src/openings.rs#L18)<br>[`bin/outbe-ocomp/src/input_artifacts.rs:1248`](../bin/outbe-ocomp/src/input_artifacts.rs#L1248)<br>[`bin/outbe-ocomp/src/input_inventory.rs:176`](../bin/outbe-ocomp/src/input_inventory.rs#L176)<br>[`bin/outbe-ocomp/src/input_inventory.rs:473`](../bin/outbe-ocomp/src/input_inventory.rs#L473) | One MANDATORY_USD_ISO constant; the other seven sites write the literal 840.<br>Oracle openings and OCOMP inputs are rejected without ISO 840. This is what lets the USD rules above always find a price. |

| What | Kind | Where | Change / note |
|---|---|---|---|
| Reference currency set and the only priced pair | Default | [`crates/system/oracle/src/genesis.rs:113`](../crates/system/oracle/src/genesis.rs#L113)<br>[`crates/system/oracle/src/genesis.rs:115`](../crates/system/oracle/src/genesis.rs#L115)<br>[`scripts/seed_genesis.py:1527`](../scripts/seed_genesis.py#L1527)<br>[`scripts/seed_genesis.py:1536`](../scripts/seed_genesis.py#L1536)<br>[`scripts/seed_genesis.py:1544`](../scripts/seed_genesis.py#L1544) | Genesis config<br>Six reference currencies (CNY 156, HKD 344, JPY 392, GBP 826, USD 840, EUR 978), but only the COEN/840 pair and a USD policy rate (3.63%) are seeded, so the other five cannot be priced until their pairs are added. ## Duplicate definitions

Values defined in more than one place. **Values differ** rows are conflicts; the rest are copies to consolidate into the suggested home.

### Protocol economics copied across crates

| Value | Status | Copies | Keep in / note |
|---|---|---|---|
| Call evaluation window (28 days) | Same value | [`crates/core/nod/src/constants.rs:45`](../crates/core/nod/src/constants.rs#L45)<br>[`crates/core/intexfactory/src/constants.rs:63`](../crates/core/intexfactory/src/constants.rs#L63)<br>[`crates/core/gem/src/constants.rs:24`](../crates/core/gem/src/constants.rs#L24)<br>[`crates/core/credis/src/constants.rs:37`](../crates/core/credis/src/constants.rs#L37) | One call-terms module in crates/core/common<br>Nod and Credis also define CALL_LOOKBACK_DAYS = 28 (nod/constants.rs:35, credis/constants.rs:26). |
| Call breach threshold (21 days) | Same value | [`crates/core/nod/src/constants.rs:50`](../crates/core/nod/src/constants.rs#L50)<br>[`crates/core/intexfactory/src/constants.rs:65`](../crates/core/intexfactory/src/constants.rs#L65)<br>[`crates/core/gem/src/constants.rs:29`](../crates/core/gem/src/constants.rs#L29)<br>[`crates/core/credis/src/constants.rs:41`](../crates/core/credis/src/constants.rs#L41) | Same call-terms module<br>Plus CALL_THRESHOLD_DAYS = 21 in nod/constants.rs:41 and credis/constants.rs:33. |
| Call notice period (7 days) | Same value | [`crates/core/nod/src/constants.rs:54`](../crates/core/nod/src/constants.rs#L54)<br>[`crates/core/intexfactory/src/constants.rs:60`](../crates/core/intexfactory/src/constants.rs#L60)<br>[`crates/core/gem/src/constants.rs:33`](../crates/core/gem/src/constants.rs#L33)<br>[`crates/core/credis/src/constants.rs:46`](../crates/core/credis/src/constants.rs#L46) | Same call-terms module |
| Floor rate (8%) | Same value | [`crates/core/nod/src/constants.rs:28`](../crates/core/nod/src/constants.rs#L28)<br>[`crates/core/intexfactory/src/constants.rs:55`](../crates/core/intexfactory/src/constants.rs#L55)<br>[`crates/core/gem/src/constants.rs:40`](../crates/core/gem/src/constants.rs#L40) | Same call-terms module<br>Two names for one value: FLOOR_RATE_PCT in Nod, FLOOR_RATE in Intex and Gem. |
